This was followed by [Claws of Gix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Claws+of+Gix), [Repeal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Repeal), [Claws of Gix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Claws+of+Gix), [Hatching Plans](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hatching+Plans), [Pyromancer's Swath](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer%27s+Swath) and [Grapeshot](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grapeshot) stormed 6 times, each copy dealing 3 due to the Swath and every copy aimed at Stuart.

Daniel wins

1-1

**Game 3 - Stuart on the play**

Stuart looked at his opening hand and mulliganed once again. Then he did so a second time. Would there be a game where each player started with 7 cards in their hand? Certainly not this one as Stuart mulliganed for a third time and unhappily kept his 4 card hand. He looked a little happier as Daniel decided to mulligan his 7 as well but not much.

"It would be handy if you mulliganed to 4"

But Daniel decided to keep his 6. Stuart's 4 card hand managed to make a [Magus of the Bazaar](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magus+of+the+Bazaar) on turn 3, after sacrificing a [Horizon Canopy](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Horizon+Canopy) on turn 2 but Daniel had the [Repeal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Repeal) for it. However, he had nothing for when Stuart replayed it on turn 4 and Stuart started to get some cards into the graveyard, dredging a [Stinkweed Imp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stinkweed+Imp) and putting a [Narcomoeba](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Narcomoeba) into play. He added a [Pithing Needle](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pithing+Needle) naming [Martyr of Ashes](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Martyr+of+Ashes) to secure his team but his second Magus was met with a [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand).

Daniel in the meantime had managed to store 2 charge counters on a [Fungal Reaches](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fungal+Reaches) and in his turn removed the counters and began announcing a storm count. [Rite of Flame](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Rite+of+Flame), then another [Rite of Flame](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Rite+of+Flame), then a third one! He now had 8 red mana in pool and a storm count of 3, he cast a [Pyromancer's Swath](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer%27s+Swath) and an [Empty the Warrens](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Empty+the+Warrens) stormed 5 times followed by a [Grapeshot](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grapeshot) stormed 6 times targeting Stuart 4 times, one of his goblins in an attempt to remove the 3 copies of [Bridge from Below](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bridge+from+Below) in the graveyard and 1 copy targeting [Magus of the Bazaar](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magus+of+the+Bazaar). However, he had done the last 2 copies in the wrong order so the Magus died, giving 3 zombie tokens, then the goblin died removing the [Bridge from Below](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bridge+from+Below).

Stuart was perched at a precarious 7 life and played a replacement Magus. Daniel's attack took him down to 1 life and left 6 goblins facing 3 zombies and 1 Magus, giving Stuart one more turn but with only colourless mana available as his [Yavimaya Coast](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Yavimaya+Coast) was no longer so handy. Stuart untapped and went to work, growing his graveyard, desperately looking for the last [Bridge from Below](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bridge+from+Below) so that he could create enough blockers. Magus activated and dredged, draw step was replaced with a dredge and Stuart even flashed back a [Bonded Fetch](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bonded+Fetch) and dredged again but all he could find were 2 measly [Narcomoeba](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Narcomoeba)s.

Daniel wins

2-1 to Daniel

**Game 4 - Stuart on the play**

Finally! Both players kept their 7 card hands.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/63/8b/638b101738f041693f11b30dd113b213276340c3a61d8c25fa86c6263b56fd60.jpg)*Stuart's graveyard - dredge much?*

Stuart opened up with a [Pithing Needle](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pithing+Needle) naming [Martyr of Ashes](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Martyr+of+Ashes) and a [Magus of the Bazaar](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magus+of+the+Bazaar). Daniel had to be content with a [Hatching Plans](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hatching+Plans). The Magus activated, putting no dredge cards into the graveyard but placing a [Dread Return](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dread+Return) there and Stuart added a [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter) and a [Drowned Rusalka](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Drowned+Rusalka) to the board, certainly not short of discard outlets here but no dredge cards.

Daniel suspended a [Lotus Bloom](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Bloom) and charged his [Fungal Reaches](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fungal+Reaches) and after Stuart found no dredge cards on his turn and added another Looter, he played [Claws of Gix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Claws+of+Gix), sacrificed his [Hatching Plans](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hatching+Plans) and made a second one. Stuart started drawing and discarding as quickly as he could and eventually found a [Golgari Grave-Troll](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Golgari+Grave-Troll) and a [Stinkweed Imp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stinkweed+Imp) in one Magus activation. Typical, just like buses, you spend ages looking for your dredge card then 2 turn up at once.

Once he had the dredge card, Stuart began to fill up his graveyard, finding a [Bridge from Below](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bridge+from+Below), then a second. Stuart sacrificed his team of discarders to flash back [Dread Return](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dread+Return) and return [Flame-Kin Zealot](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flame-Kin+Zealot) to play but Daniel had the [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand) for it. Stuart still got the Zombies however and ditched 2 of his newly earned tokens and his last remaining non-zombie creature, [Drowned Rusalka](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Drowned+Rusalka), to play another [Dread Return](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dread+Return) on the Zealot, gaining 3 new zombies giving a total of 7 3/3 haste zombies and a Zealot to attack for the win.

Stuart wins

2-2

**Game 5 - Daniel on the play**

So, down to the final game. Daniel quickly kept his 7 but Stuart wasn't so lucky, sending his first hand back. He drew his 6 card hand and looked at it for a long time. Finally he decided to keep and played a Gemstone Cavern before the first turn.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/dd/7e/dd7eb3c4eae78c6e614eab2930627499eabc0deb76c06f8f8004efdef62b7920.jpg)

On the actual first turn, Daniel suspended a [Lotus Bloom](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Bloom). Stuart made the best use of his Gemstone Cavern to fire out a [Magus of the Bazaar](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magus+of+the+Bazaar) on his first turn but Daniel had the [Grapeshot](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grapeshot) for it. Stuart made a [Bonded Fetch](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bonded+Fetch) on his second turn though and Daniel seemed to have no answer, as he passed the turn back. At the end of turn, Stuart drew and discarded a [Golgari Grave-Troll](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Golgari+Grave-Troll) and started to dredge. 2 [Narcomoeba](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Narcomoeba)s jumped into play and Stuart attempted to cast a [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter) which received a [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand).

On Daniel's fourth turn, the [Lotus Bloom](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Bloom) arrived. He sacrificed it for red mana and cast a [Rite of Flame](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Rite+of+Flame), then a [Perilous Research](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Perilous+Research), losing a land, then a [Claws of Gix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Claws+of+Gix) and finally an [Empty the Warrens](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Empty+the+Warrens) for 10 goblins. [Claws of Gix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Claws+of+Gix) and the tokens now gave Stuart the problem that his [Bridge from Below](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bridge+from+Below) was not going to hang around in his graveyard for long if he dredged one but Daniel had used most of his cards to get to this position and now only had 2 left.

After dredging a bunch more cards to the graveyard and another [Narcomoeba](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Narcomoeba) into play, Stuart played a [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter) and passed. Daniel attacked Stuart for 10, down to 6, and passed the turn back, 3 land untapped and giving Stuart [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand) to think about.

Stuart untapped and by the end of his draw phase, his graveyard occupied about 50% of the width of his side of the play mat, laid out in 4 columns. His fourth [Narcomoeba](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Narcomoeba) had also made its way into play. Stuart shrugged his shoulders and declared "well, if you have a [Remand](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Remand)…" He sacrificed 3 of his 6 creatures in play flashing back [Dread Return](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dread+Return) targeting [Golgari Grave-Troll](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Golgari+Grave-Troll), provoking Daniel to sacrifice a goblin token to [Claws of Gix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Claws+of+Gix) to remove the 3 copies of [Bridge from Below](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bridge+from+Below) from the game and taking Daniel back up to 18 life. The Grave-Troll hit play as a 16/16 and Stuart ditched his other 3 non-troll creature and attempted to flashback another [Dread Return](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dread+Return) targeting [Flame-Kin Zealot](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flame-Kin+Zealot). He looked up at Daniel but he simply nodded his assent for it to resolve.

Stuart now had a 17/17 [Golgari Grave-Troll](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Golgari+Grave-Troll) and a 3/3 [Flame-Kin Zealot](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flame-Kin+Zealot), both with haste and both entered the red zone. Daniel looked down at his life total and then down at his lands. 20 points of attacking power, 18 life and only 2 lands untapped for the [Claws of Gix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Claws+of+Gix). Shaking his head regretfully, he extended his hand.

**Stuart Wright wins game 5 and the match beating Daniel Godfrey 3-2**

  

## Sunday, 3:47 p.m. - Finals: Craig Jones vs. Stuart Wright

*by Tim Willoughby*

 

"You might think that you have a good matchup here… but so did the other two"

![](https://i.ormos.uk/56/1f/561f7f1a6cad9e163a5e5d0814d9e69e510023f82ed66e012471a2b07fe818d5.jpg)

Stuart Wright had a pretty tempestuous top eight, facing the nightmare matchup of Rakdos in the quarter-finals, and prevailing against the odds, then squeaking through in the semis, where he found that [Claws of Gix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Claws+of+Gix) plus [Empty the Warrens](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Empty+the+Warrens) is pretty frustrating. This finals of GB Nationals might be one of the most impressive seen in the UK in recent memory. Both players are members of the Mox Radio team, and have tested together, but are also known as regulars on the Pro Tour, and will prove formidable foes in the team portion of Worlds in New York.

For the finals, he won the die roll, and it was Dredge going first against Red Deck Wins 2007. We won't Dan Paskins about the green cards. The last time that Craig Jones hoisted a trophy

As Stuart was forced to mulligan, Craig remarked that he had found that happened a lot when he tested it too.

"I kept doing it last match. It didn't seem to matter…"

Stuart led off with a [Breeding Pool](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Breeding+Pool), while Craig had a turn one [Mogg Fanatic](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mogg+Fanatic), suggesting already that a [Bridge from Below](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bridge+from+Below) plan might not work too well.

For turn two, Stuart hard cast a [Narcomoeba](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Narcomoeba) off a [Horizon Canopy](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Horizon+Canopy), and Craig chuckled a while. He had a [Seal of Fire](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Seal+of+Fire), and a [Treetop Village](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Treetop+Village). A [Gemstone Mine](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gemstone+Mine) into [Drowned Rusalka](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Drowned+Rusalka) for Stuart met a response of [Seal of Fire](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Seal+of+Fire) on [Narcomoeba](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Narcomoeba) - Craig did not want to let the dredge engine ever get started.

The next turn, he attacked with [Mogg Fanatic](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mogg+Fanatic), to which Stuart blocked and, after [Mogg Fanatic](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mogg+Fanatic) had gone to the dome, he sacrificed [Drowned Rusalka](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Drowned+Rusalka), dredging a [Golgari Grave-Troll](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Golgari+Grave-Troll). A dredge in his draw step meant that Stuart had a [Bridge from Below](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bridge+from+Below), a few dredgers, and a [Dread Return](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dread+Return) in his graveyard.

[Bridge from Below](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bridge+from+Below) did not stay in Stuart's graveyard for long, as it was removed by a [Mogg War Marshal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mogg+War+Marshal) not having echo paid, and Stuart playd a [Stinkweed Imp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stinkweed+Imp), seemingly ready to ignore the Bridge plan. [Life from the Loam](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Life+from+the+Loam) built up Stuart's land base, and he had a [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter) to potentially fill his graveyard further. [Tarmogoyf](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tarmogoyf) on the other side of the board came down as a massive threat, but was effectively turned off by [Stinkweed Imp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stinkweed+Imp), at least initially.

Craig only had 2 lands still. He attacked with a pair of Craig Jones goblin tokens, taking Stuart to 12. In his upkeep, [Merfolk Looter](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Merfolk+Looter) dredged back a [Stinkweed Imp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stinkweed+Imp), making the graveyard larger, but not particularly more scary. The dredged [Golgari Grave-Troll](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Golgari+Grave-Troll) in the draw step did put another Bridge there though, and a [Svogthos, the Restless Tomb](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Svogthos%2C+the+Restless+Tomb), that could at some point be brought back with Life From the Loam.

Stuart cast a 12/12 [Golgari Grave-Troll](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Golgari+Grave-Troll).

"Here's where you start Incinerating me…"

The first [Incinerate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Incinerate) took Stuart to 10, while Craig was on 16. A pair of Craig Jones tokens ran in, and Stuart gave up his [Bridge from Below](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bridge+from+Below)s by blocking and killing each. Jones finally had a third land, but it was a [Treetop Village](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Treetop+Village), which was both a little slow, and meant he didn't have a non-painful source of red. What Craig *did* have was a [Mogg War Marshal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mogg+War+Marshal), to provide some much needed blockers.

From nowhere, on the following turn, Stuart got an air force of [Narcomoeba](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Narcomoeba)s from his dredging, and must suddenly have been contemplating a big [Dread Return](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dread+Return), which had not been on the cards before.

"This game's easy… I haven't drawn any lands and have basically done nothing" declared Craig, who was tapped out and facing down a potent force.

Meanwhile, Stuart was doing some math and trying to work out if he could go for it this turn. In the end he just went in with his [Stinkweed Imp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stinkweed+Imp), and cast another [Golgari Grave-Troll](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Golgari+Grave-Troll), who was a 17/17.  
 "My Troll is bigger than you!" taunted Stu, pretty happy with his position.

"Yeah, my [Tarmogoyf](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tarmogoyf) is feeling a little puny."

For his turn, Craig dropped Stomping Grounds, and played another [Tarmogoyf](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tarmogoyf) before suspending two [Rift Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Rift+Bolt)s. Craig was now on 11. Stuart was looking to get a Bridge From Below into his graveyard on his dredges, such that he could swarm around Craig's creatures. The regular dredge didn't get there. He had to sacrifice [Horizon Canopy](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Horizon+Canopy), to dredge a [Stinkweed Imp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stinkweed+Imp), finding that Bridge From Below was the last card in his deck. Once it was there though, he flashed back multiple [Dread Return](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dread+Return)s on [Flame-Kin Zealot](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flame-Kin+Zealot), and easily got there.

Stuart Wright wins game 1

**Game 2**

For game 2, Stuart was running scared of [Magus of the Moon](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magus+of+the+Moon). Without any basic land, or red spells, he had no answer to it. Craig had a turn one suspend on [Greater Gargadon](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Greater+Gargadon), Craig followed up with a pair of [Seal of Fire](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Seal+of+Fire).  
 "No enablers here…"

![](https://i.ormos.uk/71/b8/71b8fddbd63a9788cf6adfb759538692b42083c5974377a1b93c247581419bfb.jpg)

Stuart did have one dredger though, in the form of Life From the Loam, without targets. Now Stuart would have to dredge the old fashioned way, as his [Mountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mountain)s would not do the trick. That first dredge didn't get him to another dredger, and he proceeded to take his first points of beats from Craig's team. The game was not going to take long, with [Mogg War Marshal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mogg+War+Marshal) fueling the Gargadon, and more burn spells coming from Prof, who had turned Stuart into a red mage, albeit one without any red spells.

Craig Jones wins game 2

**Game 3**

"It's all good… I lost the second game both other rounds too"

The entirety of game 3 can be covered in a single haiku.

Stuart kept on six cards  
 Craig's draw was a turn 3 win  
[Magus of the Moon](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magus+of+the+Moon)

Craig Jones wins game 3.

**Game 4**

![](https://i.ormos.uk/fc/22/fc2238839086eecd2d99d513345f0678ed61c9a2dec14763d259114f58768093.jpg)*Stuart Wright finishes in 2nd Place*

Craig led with a [Seal of Fire](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Seal+of+Fire) on turn 1, while Stuart had a [Wall of Roots](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Roots), into [Llanowar Mentor](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Llanowar+Mentor). The Elf Spellshaper died to Seal on Craig's turn, in order that the [Tarmogoyf](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tarmogoyf) that Craig cast could attack for more.

Stuart had a [Bonded Fetch](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bonded+Fetch), the little enabler that could, but Craig had a [Magus of the Moon](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magus+of+the+Moon). Having [Wall of Roots](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Roots) meant that this time Stuart wasn't immediately dead, and play continued. [Bonded Fetch](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bonded+Fetch) dredged a [Stinkweed Imp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stinkweed+Imp), and then discarded it again. In the draw step it again jumped into Stuart's hand, revealing a Bridge From Below to keep Stuart in the fight. Stuart cast another [Wall of Roots](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Roots), and cast Life From the Loam to fetch back 3 lands. Stuart would potentially be able to cast [Golgari Grave-Troll](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Golgari+Grave-Troll)s, and flash back [Dread Return](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dread+Return)s in spite of none of his lands being able to tap for useful coloured mana.

One of Stuart's two copies of [Wall of Roots](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Roots) got hit by [Incinerate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Incinerate), and replaced by a Zombie token thanks to [Bridge from Below](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bridge+from+Below). He then went to 11 from a [Tarmogoyf](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tarmogoyf) attack, and found that his [Bonded Fetch](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bonded+Fetch) would be made impotent by virtue of a [Pithing Needle](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pithing+Needle). Stuart got in with his Zombie, confident that [Magus of the Moon](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Magus+of+the+Moon) would not be a blocker, but was in huge trouble the very next turn, when [Mogg War Marshal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mogg+War+Marshal) and [Greater Gargadon](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Greater+Gargadon) did a number on [Bridge from Below](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bridge+from+Below).

It was only a turn later that Stuart scooped it up.

Craig Jones won the game and the match.

Congratulations to Craig Jones, the Great British National Champion!

![](https://i.ormos.uk/68/6f/686f41fcd9f074bf4e6d57c97be392dd205a16743d12e3f18c69857310695b22.jpg)  

## Sunday, 5:00 p.m. - Vintage Nationals Finals

*by Tim Willoughby*

  
![](https://i.ormos.uk/4c/71/4c71c31dd68e577ffcec6e7f538f2d2f75769f63bdb83c3c0de941aa8dad41ff.jpg)*Lee Wood*

After some frantic play with decks that take longer to type up than a [Mirari's Wake](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mirari%27s+Wake) mirror takes to play, it all comes down to two players from the Hemel games club battling for the title of Great Britain Vintage National Champion.

Lee MJB Wood Esq. III piloted Fish, while Jim Underwood had 7/10 split.

On the play, Jim had a mulligan, but a turn one [Trinisphere](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Trinisphere) off a Mishra's Workshop. This kept Lee from playing a great deal before turn three, while Jim tapped his Workshop for a Mox Emerald, but by the time he had Goblin Welder out, Lee had enough mana to tap 3 for a Swords to Plowshares.

Lee played a [True Believer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=True+Believer) with a smile, but it turned into a frown when Karn, Silver Golem came out the following turn. A Mana Vault and a Bazaar of Baghdad joined his side of the board soon after. The Bazaar got hit by Wasteland before ever being activated, and each player sat across from each other inactive for some time, until Jim tried for [Memnarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Memnarch), only for it to get hit by Force of Will.

Lee had an [Isamaru, Hound of Konda](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Isamaru%2C+Hound+of+Konda), while Jim built up his mana further with a [Gilded Lotus](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gilded+Lotus). His Mishra's Workshop got hit by Wasteland, but Jim won a small victory back, killing True Beliver when it attacked by animating his [Gilded Lotus](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gilded+Lotus) with Karn, making a hefty blocker. Lee summoned [Jotun Grunt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jotun+Grunt), but was still a little stuck under the weight of [Trinisphere](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Trinisphere). Each player had a number of Brainstorms, but with more mana, it was Underwood who could best profit from the cards.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/cc/a2/cca2603ba9c852e795ce9de6c8084627547de8f5433e8de805361b5660d45730.jpg)*Jim Underwood*

[Meddling Mage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Meddling+Mage) from Lee turned off Tinker, but Lee was soon in trouble in a whole new way, when [Triskelion](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Triskelion) reared its metallic head. Mana Vault was very slowly killing Jim, but he looked in prime position to start killing Lee, with [Jotun Grunt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jotun+Grunt)'s cumulative upkeep finishing it off, and only a pair of 2/2 creature left to block with.

A [Chalice of the Void](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chalice+of+the+Void) set at 2 resolved, and it seemed that Lee was virtually locked. Only at this point did Jim look to attack. His first attempt at animating [Gilded Lotus](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gilded+Lotus) was [Stifle](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stifle)d, and again the turn was passed. It wasn't until his next attack step that Lee finally took some damage, when [Triskelion](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Triskelion) got through unblocked. A [Panoptic Mirror](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Panoptic+Mirror) after combat threatened both getting into the red zone, and perhaps something awkward like lots and lots of Time Walks.

[Overwhelm](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Overwhelm)ingly behind, and with enough big artifacts on the board to kill him, Lee scooped it up.

**Game 2**

Lee kept what looked to be a pretty solid hand including both [Kataki, War's Wage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kataki%2C+War%27s+Wage) and Null Rod. He then faced down a turn one Tinker for [Triskelion](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Triskelion). The Rod turned [Triskelion](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Triskelion) into 'just a 4/4', but that still put Lee on a 5 turn clock. Jim had a [Chalice of the Void](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chalice+of+the+Void) for one, but Lee brought down Kataki, forcing Jim to use most of his mana on just staying where he was. Wasteland from Lee took down Tolarian Academy, and each players creatures took some time to do a little bashing.

[Disenchant](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disenchant) from Lee killed of [Triskelion](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Triskelion), and suddenly it looked that Lee might win the race. Jim let his Chalice go, and then played a Goblin Welder. Lee had Old Man of the Sea to steal it. Jim put a Mox Jet down, but it got hit by Force of Will, from Lee who was wary of the one turn window where the Welder was active. The Welder attacked and was beaten up by the Old Man.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/9c/bd/9cbd0b92b30851a665835320be1ea6bfbfa9b7d316fc03c10dd2c46d720bac36.jpg)

Old Man beats came straight for Jim the turn after, and Lee's team was joined by a [Jotun Grunt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jotun+Grunt). Attacks from Lee the following turn left Jim hoping for a good draw. It didn't come though, and the match went to 1-1.

**Game 3**

Game 3 began with a turn one [Trinisphere](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Trinisphere) from Jim, and an exclamation of despair from Lee. With a Mana Vault, Jim soon had a [Triskelion](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Triskelion) in play, but was trumped again by a Null Rod from Lee. [Kataki, War's Wage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kataki%2C+War%27s+Wage) was stopped by Force of Will, but Jim was now taking fair amounts of damage, from Ancient Tomb and Mana Vault, while not dishing out too much in return. [Jotun Grunt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jotun+Grunt)s stalled the ground, as Jim dropped to 8 from his Mana Vault. Jim had [Words of War](https://web.archive.org/web/20170719033333/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Words+of+War), while Lee just had Old Man of the Sea. The Old Man attacked Jim to 3, and when he drew the next turn it was all over.

Congratulations to Lee MJB Wood Esquire Ford Escort III Bsc Ssc Gsc - Great Britain's Vintage National Champion!
